### [PAR-18] P1/dead-control/S Yatra planning CTA opens WhatsApp to the placeholder number 919999999999
  - where: apps/mobile/app/(devotee)/yatra-detail.tsx:49
  - steps: 1. Open a yatra destination (not a package) in the app and tap the book/plan CTA.
2. Read yatra-detail.tsx:46-50: when `!isPackage`, onBook does Linking.openURL(`https://wa.me/919999999999?text=...`).
  - observed: The hardcoded number is 919999999999 — a placeholder, not a real business line. There is no env var or config behind it. For destinations (as opposed to packages) this is the only booking path on the screen.
  - expected: A real WhatsApp business number sourced from config, or an in-app inquiry form posting to /api/v1/yatra-inquiry (which exists and is registered at server.ts:421).
  - customer_impact: A high-intent yatra lead taps Plan My Yatra, WhatsApp opens to a nonexistent contact, and the lead is lost with no trace in the CRM. Yatra is one of the highest-ticket SKUs in the catalogue.
  - fix: Move the number to EXPO_PUBLIC_WHATSAPP_NUMBER, or better, post to /api/v1/yatra-inquiry so the lead is captured server-side regardless of whether WhatsApp opens.

### [PAR-19] P1/dead-control/M Admin Settings feature-flag switches are local state only — toggling a payment gateway or video-proof enforcement does nothing
  - where: apps/mobile/app/(admin)/settings.tsx:22-33
  - steps: 1. Log in as admin@demo.com and open the mobile Admin > Settings screen.
2. Toggle 'Video Proof Required' or 'Razorpay Domestic' off.
3. Navigate away and back.
  - observed: `const [state, setState] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({video_proof_required:true, nri_pricing:true, razorpay:true, stripe:true, ai_jyotish:false, auto_approve:true})` and `function toggle(id){ setState(s => ({...s,[id]:!s[id]})) }` — no API call anywhere in the file, no persistence. The state resets on unmount. Note it also still carries a `stripe: true` flag although the Stripe rail was removed from the API.
  - expected: Each switch reads and writes a real platform flag (there is an /api/v1/admin/ops surface registered at server.ts:381) and shows the persisted value.
  - customer_impact: An admin can believe they have disabled a payment gateway, turned off mandatory video proof for payouts, or stopped auto-approving devotee accounts — and nothing changed. That is a false sense of operational control over money and trust-and-safety settings.
  - fix: Back the switches with a real settings endpoint, or remove the screen until one exists. Never ship a control that looks authoritative and is a no-op. Drop the dead `stripe` flag.

### [PAR-20] P1/dead-control/M Admin Reports "Generate" button fires no request but tells the admin an email is coming
  - where: apps/mobile/app/(admin)/reports.tsx:14-22 (six hardcoded reports) and :37-49 (exportReport)
  - steps: 1. Log in as admin@demo.com, open mobile Admin > Reports.
2. Tap any report (e.g. 'GST Statement'), then tap Generate.
  - observed: exportReport shows a confirm Alert, and its Generate onPress calls `Alert.alert('Queued', 'Your report is being generated. You will receive an email shortly.')` — there is no fetch, no mutation, no queue write anywhere in the file. The six report definitions are a hardcoded array. (This screen additionally crashes at line 62 on `dash.totalBookings.toLocaleString` — see PAR-04.)
  - expected: Generate enqueues a real export job and the admin receives the file.
  - customer_impact: An admin waits for a GST statement or refunds ledger that will never arrive, and has no way to tell it failed. At tax time that is a compliance problem, not just a UX one.
  - fix: Either wire the six exports to real endpoints (admin-donations-export.ts already exists for one of them) or hide the screen behind a flag until they do.

### [PAR-21] P2/wrong-data/S The same darshan stream reports a different status and a different playback URL from two endpoints
  - where: apps/api/src/routes/darshan.ts:223 (GET /live) vs :380 (GET /:id/stream)
  - steps: 1. GET http://127.0.0.1:4000/api/v1/darshan/live and note streams[0].status and streams[0].playbackUrl.
2. GET http://127.0.0.1:4000/api/v1/darshan/60a66677-2dd0-487c-a418-6ad91da483d1/stream and note status and playbackUrl for the same id.
  - observed: /live -> status "live", playbackUrl "https://stream.darshya.online/kashi-vishwanath-varanasi/live.m3u8". /:id/stream -> status "offline", playbackUrl "https://stream.darshya.online/kashi-vishwanath". Same stream id, two different states and two different URLs. The website's /darshan page renders it as 'LIVE ... Live Now 1 stream'.
  - expected: Both endpoints derive status and playbackUrl from the same source of truth.
  - customer_impact: A listing says a temple is live; opening it says offline and points at a different (probably 404ing) manifest. Once PAR-05 is fixed, this becomes the next visible break in the same flow — the player would load the wrong URL. Also note the mobile player hardcodes a red 'LIVE' badge in the header (darshan-player.tsx) regardless of the real status.
  - fix: Have /:id/stream reuse the same status/URL derivation as /live, and drive the player's LIVE badge from the returned status.

### [PAR-22] P2/parity/L Provider earnings and temple payouts/bookings screens exist on web but are missing from the app; the app has no search and almost no content library
  - where: apps/mobile/app/(temple)/, (astrologer)/, (wellness)/ vs apps/web/app/temple-portal/, astrologer-portal/, wellness-portal/; apps/mobile/app/(devotee)/index.tsx:113 vs apps/web/app/search/page.tsx
  - steps: 1. Compare the file listings: ls apps/mobile/app/(temple) vs ls apps/web/app/temple-portal.
2. Repeat for astrologer and wellness.
3. In the app, tap the home search bar (index.tsx:110-116).
  - observed: Web temple-portal has bookings, payouts and schedule pages; the mobile (temple) group has none of them. Web astrologer-portal/earnings and wellness-portal/earnings have no mobile equivalent — those providers cannot see what they are owed on mobile at all. The home search bar does not search: onPress is `router.push('/(devotee)/pujas')`, so it is a disguised tab link. Web ships ~150 content/SEO routes (gita, ramayana, vedas, upanishads, chalisa, aarti, shlokas, deity, nakshatra, tithi, vrat, ekadashi, jyotirlinga, shakti-peeth...) plus ~20 astrology tools (kundli-milan, numerology, manglik, sade-sati, kaal-sarp, dasha, choghadiya, hora, rahu-kalam, vastu, baby-name...); the app has content.tsx plus a knowledge hub gated off by default (knowledge.tsx:22, EXPO_PUBLIC_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED) and only kundli + horoscope natively — everything else is a Linking.openURL to darshya.online. Web-only with no mobile screen at all: cart/checkout, referrals (/refer), gift a puja (/gift), antim-sanskar, matchmaking, csr-match, org/B2B, provider KYC, recurring puja subscriptions, yatra companion/plan. Mobile admin has 8 screens vs 19 on web (missing agents, leads, outreach, roadmap, ratings, waitlist, metrics, consultations, subscriptions, b2b-onboarding).
  - expected: A documented, deliberate scope for v1 mobile — with the money-facing provider screens (earnings, payouts) included, since those are the ones providers check daily.
  - customer_impact: Astrologers and wellness instructors have no way to see their earnings in the app, and temple managers cannot see bookings or request payouts. The app markets itself as the product but is a thin, partly-fake shell over a website users are constantly bounced out to. A search bar that silently navigates to a tab instead of searching is a small but constant credibility tax.
  - fix: Decide the v1 mobile scope explicitly. At minimum add the three earnings/payouts screens (the /payouts and /*-portal endpoints already exist and return 200), make the home search bar either search or look like a link, and replace the eleven Linking.openURL bounce-outs with in-app WebViews so the session and back-stack survive.

## NARRATIVE
# DARSHYA — LAUNCH READINESS SYNTHESIS
**Date:** 2026-08-12 · **Evidence base:** 11 agents, real authenticated browser sessions across all 8 roles against a fully seeded stack · **166 raw findings**, deduplicated below · **One area (app↔web parity) is contract-verified, not click-verified** — the Expo app could not be run in this environment; its findings come from source inspection plus live API probes of all 133 method+path pairs, with the two render crashes reproduced in Node against real API payloads.

---

## 1. LAUNCH SCORE

# **36 / 100 — NO-GO**

| Dimension | Score | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Core flows | **7 / 25** | Of six revenue flows, one (puja booking) works up to the gateway. Store checkout is 100% blocked by two stacked defects. Pandit fulfilment is 100% blocked at the first click. Astrology captures money against fictional providers. Wellness has a complete supply side and literally zero demand side. Donations are the only flow that runs clean end to end. |
| Auth + roles | **7 / 10** | Genuinely strong: 111 admin routes probed with a forged devotee token → all 401/403; cross-role IDOR on a real booking → 403 for all 7 non-owners; rate limiting fires at 10 attempts; CORS rejects evil origins. Deductions: `astro@demo.com` 500s on every route, and three provider roles (store, temple, gaushala) have no profile row so their portals are empty shells. |
| Money correctness | **6 / 15** | The *arithmetic* is excellent — ₹1,100 held identical across 6 surfaces down to `amount:115100` paise; 20/80 verified against the Commission ledger on both astrology and wellness. The *movement* is broken: payouts are never minted, commissions are never reversed on refund, GST is dropped server-side, subscriptions are granted free, and the headline revenue tile is structurally ₹0. |
| UI/UX + design consistency | **5 / 15** | 11 primary-button heights, 4 radii, 2 competing "primary" colours, 3 of 4 font tokens dead at runtime, 15 dead controls on the two highest-intent catalogues. |
| Content & trust / legal | **2 / 10** | Three published refund ladders + a fourth in the code that actually spends the money. A live 80G tax receipt issued for a donee with no 80G registration and no PAN. A public page claiming CSR-1 registration. No legal entity, GSTIN, address or named grievance officer anywhere. |
| Performance & SEO | **5 / 10** | *Largely unmeasured this run — the biggest blind spot in this report.* Inferred deductions only: no `h1` on the login page or the puja error boundary, heading-level skips on the two catalogue pages, filtered/sorted views never reach the URL (zero shareable or indexable facet pages), empty blog index, `X-Powered-By` exposed, no CSP. |
| Mobile / responsive | **2 / 5** | Layout engineering is genuinely good — **zero horizontal overflow across 30 page × viewport combinations**. Undone by one z-order defect that buries the login form and the entire bottom tab bar on first visit, plus 17–44 sub-44px tap targets per page and 190 sub-12px text elements on the mobile homepage. |
| Ops readiness | **2 / 10** | An operator can approve a provider and read a commission ledger. They cannot pay anyone, refund anyone, review KYC, block a fraudster, open a user record, or trust any revenue number on screen. Settings "Save" is a 700ms `setTimeout`. |

### Defending the number against the prior audits (41 → ~74 → 36)

The ~74 was almost certainly right *on the evidence it had*. Code-level audits confirm that endpoints exist, that 1059/1059 API tests pass, that the state machine is guarded, and that the money math is correct — and all of that is still true today. The security agent independently scored 79 on adversarial probing, which corroborates that the backend is sound.

**36 is what happens when you click instead of read.** The failures are almost entirely in the seam between a correct UI and a correct API:

- `PATCH` with `Content-Type: application/json` and no body → Fastify 400. No unit test covers "does the button send a body."
- A React screen reading `.data` on a payload keyed `{temples}` → renders a hardcoded mock instead of an error. TypeScript passed because the interface was fabricated.
- A page calling `/bookings/my` with no `limit` → API's default 20 silently truncates 33 rows, and the tab count is computed off the truncated array so the UI reports "All 20" as the total.
- A `<select>` in a server component with no `name`, no `onChange` and no enclosing `<form>` → renders perfectly, filters nothing, throws no error.

None of these produce a stack trace, a failing test, or a red console. They are invisible to every form of verification except a human clicking. That gap — roughly 38 points — is the entire distance between 74 and 36.

**Verdict: NO-GO.** Not because the product is far away — Wave 1 is about 9–12 focused days — but because today a paying customer can pay for a puja and not find it in My Bookings, cannot buy anything from the store at all, and the pandit who is supposed to serve them cannot click Accept.

---

## 2. P0 — BLOCKS LAUNCH

Ranked by "how fast does this cost you a real customer or create legal liability."

---

### **P0-1 · Store checkout is 100% impossible — two stacked, independently fatal blockers**
*Merges S-01, S-02, S-10 · Effort: **M** (backend), then **L** for the catalogue unification*

**Repro:** Log in as `devotee@demo.com` → `/store` → add any product → `/store/checkout` → COD → "Place Order — Rs 733".
1. `POST /api/v1/store/orders` → **400** `{"fieldErrors":{"shippingAddress":["Expected string, received null"]}}`. Saved addresses carry `line2: null`; the Zod schema at `apps/api/src/routes/store-orders.ts:29` is `z.string().optional()`, which rejects `null`.
2. Remove `line2` and it 500s instead: `P2023 Error creating UUID ... found 'p' at 1`. The 24 shopper-facing products carry ids like `prod_015`; `StoreProduct.id` is a UUID column. `GET /store/products/live` (the real DB catalogue) returns **0 products**.

**Impact:** Zero store revenue is collectible by any route. Orders created: 0 before, 0 after. The customer is shown a truncated raw JSON blob mid-word at the highest-intent moment in the funnel.

**Fix:** `line2: z.string().nullish()` (same for `phone`); guard the product lookup with a UUID-shape check so a bad id 400s instead of 500ing; then unify the catalogue — back `GET /store/products` with `StoreProduct` rows, or map the static handle to a real row. Replace the raw-body error render with `lib/friendly-error.ts`.

---

### **P0-2 · Pandit cannot accept, start or complete a single booking — total supply-side outage**
*PF-01 · Effort: **S** (one-line, two files)*

**Repro:** Sign in as `pandit@demo.com` → `/priest/bookings/view?id=<accepted booking>` → click "Start Puja".
→ `400 PATCH /api/v1/priest/bookings/<id>/start` · on-screen: `Action failed: API 400: {"code":"FST_ERR_CTP_EMPTY_JSON_BODY","message":"Body cannot be empty when content-type is set to application/json"}`. On the *list* page the same request fails and shows **nothing at all** (`// Silent fail for MVP`).

The identical PATCH **without** the Content-Type header returns 200. The endpoints are fine; the client request is malformed.

**Impact:** Every paid puja stalls at "Accepted" forever. No video, no delivery, no payout, no refund trigger. This is the first click a new pandit makes.

**Fix:** Drop `'Content-Type': 'application/json'` from the bodyless PATCH calls (or send `body: '{}'`) in `apps/web/app/priest/bookings/view/page.tsx:131-137` and `apps/web/app/priest/bookings/page.tsx:169-176`. Replace the list page's silent `if (res.ok)` with an error toast.

---

### **P0-3 · The pandit is never paid — proof upload mints no payout, and no UI anywhere can mark a booking delivered**
*Merges PF-02 + PF-04 · Effort: **S** backend + **S** UI*

**Repro:** Take a booking to `completed` as a pandit → upload video proof (200, status → `video_uploaded`) → open `/priest/payouts`.
Before: `{totalEarned:"770", pending:"770"}`, 1 row. After a successful proof upload on a ₹1,100 booking: **identical**, `matching: []`. Observed on a real account: **6 bookings carrying video proof, "Total Earned ₹0"** on the dashboard.

Root cause: `uploadVideoProof` does a direct `prisma.pujaBooking.update` and never reaches `updateBookingStatus`, which is the only place `paymentService.createPriestPayout` is called. Proof: `PATCH /bookings/:id {"status":"delivered"}` via curl immediately produces `{amount:"770", status:"pending", payoutMethod:"bank_transfer"}`.

And that `delivered` transition — the only one that mints the payout — **has no control in apps/web, apps/admin or apps/mobile.** Grepping all four apps returns nothing for puja bookings. It is curl-only.

**Impact:** Pandits do the work, file the proof, and see ₹0. The platform's own books have no record of what it owes. First payout cycle produces nothing and every pandit churns.

**Fix:** Call `createPriestPayout(bookingId)` at the end of `uploadVideoProof` (already idempotent and Aadhaar-gated), or route the write through `updateBookingStatus('video_uploaded')`. Add a "Confirm delivery" button on the devotee booking page (owner is already authorised at `routes/bookings.ts:285-292`) and/or "Mark delivered" in the pandit portal.

---

### **P0-4 · The booking a customer just paid for is invisible in My Bookings**
*DB-01 · Effort: **S***

**Repro:** As `devotee@demo.com` open `/bookings`. Tabs read "All 20 · Upcoming 12 · Completed 7 · Cancelled 1". The API says `{"total":33,"totalPages":2}`; `?limit=100` returns 33 rows.

The page fetches `${API_BASE_URL}/bookings/my` with **no page or limit param** (`page.tsx:191`), the server defaults to 20, and the tab counts are computed from the already-truncated array — so "All 20" is presented as the total. There is no Load-more and no page control. **Booking `BS-20260812-QXUWN` — created by clicking "Pay ₹1,151 with Razorpay" in the real UI — is in the 33 but not in the first 20, so it never appears.**

**Impact:** "You took my money and lost my booking." The customer cannot quote a booking number they cannot see, so the support loop is broken too. Every devotee past their 20th booking — exactly the repeat customers worth most — is permanently affected.

**Fix:** Request `?limit=100` (or thread `page` and render Load-more from the `totalPages` the API already returns), and derive the "All" count from the API's `total`.

---

### **P0-5 · An 80G tax receipt is issued for a donee with no 80G registration and no PAN**
*LEG-02 · Effort: **S** code, **owner-gated** for the real fix*

**Repro (executed live):** `gs_001 "Shri Krishna Gaushala"` has `pan: null`, `regn_80g: null`. `POST /donations/create-order` (₹501) → 200 → `POST /donations/verify` → **200** `{"receiptNumber":"DR-20260812-7335","status":"paid"}`.

The generated PDF prints the header **"80G Income Tax Exemption"** (`pdf-receipt.ts:85`) and the line **"Eligible for deduction under Section 80G of the Income Tax Act, 1961."** (`:156`) with **no conditional**. The registration-number and donee-PAN lines are inside `if (...)` guards and are silently omitted — so the document asserts the exemption while dropping the two fields that would substantiate it. `company.ts` says `HAS_80G_VERIFIED: false, pan: null, eightyGNumber: null`. This directly violates the project's own business rule 7.

**Impact:** Every donor receives a PDF telling them to claim a deduction the ITD will disallow. Donors face disallowance and penalty on assessment; the platform is generating false tax documents at scale, automatically, from ₹101 upward. This is the single worst legal exposure in the product.

**Fix:** Gate the 80G header and eligibility line on `gaushala80GNumber && gaushalaPan` both being present; otherwise title it "DONATION RECEIPT" with "This is an acknowledgement of your contribution. It is not an 80G tax receipt." Remove 80G advertising from `/donations` and `/gaushala` for unregistered donees.

---

### **P0-6 · The site promises 100% refund; the code pays 75% — and tells customers they get nothing when they are owed 50%**
*Merges LEG-01 + LEG-03 + DB-06 · Effort: **S** copy, **M** if rendered from the policy engine*

Four mutually contradictory refund ladders are live:
- `/refunds` table: 100% >24h · 50% 6–24h · 0% <6h
- `/terms` §8: "full refund" up to 24h
- `/faqs`, `/nri-pujas`, `/why-darshya`: inside 24h "cancellations are **not permitted**", "**non-refundable**", "**no refund**"
- **`cancellation.service.ts:47-58` (the code that moves the money):** 48h+ → 100% · 24–48h → **75%** · 4–24h → **50%** · <4h → **25%**

Plus the checkout badge directly above the Pay button reads **"100% Money-Back"** and the puja detail page reads **"100% refund if not satisfied"** — a dissatisfaction refund that exists nowhere in the code.

**Impact, both directions:** A devotee cancelling 30 hours before a ₹5,000 puja reads "100% refund" on two legal pages and is automatically refunded ₹3,750 — ₹1,250 short-paid, provably against the site's own text. And a devotee cancelling 10 hours out reads on three pages that they have no remedy, does nothing, and forfeits the ₹4,000 the engine would have paid them. Under the Consumer Protection Act 2019 the first is deficiency of service plus unfair trade practice; it is the cheapest possible complaint for a consumer to file.

**Fix:** Pick **one** ladder. Rewrite `/refunds`, `/terms` §8, `/faqs`, `/nri-pujas`, `/why-darshya` and both trust badges to state it verbatim. Best: expose `DEFAULT_CANCEL_POLICY.tiers` on a public endpoint and render the table from it so copy cannot drift again. Also resolve the internal contradiction that the 24h hard block in `booking.service.ts:850` makes the 50% and 25% tiers unreachable.

---

### **P0-7 · Astrology takes money against 15 fictional astrologers, and the real ones can never see a booking**
*Merges AW-01 + AW-02 + AW-03 · Effort: **M***

Three defects that compound into "astrology cannot deliver anything":

1. **AW-02:** `/astrology` lists 15 fixture astrologers. Booking one succeeds — consultation `05723e03…`, reference `AC-20260812-3F1C15`, ₹750 captured, room minted, success screen says "Your astrologer has been notified." The admin commission ledger has **no row** for it; `consultation.ts:433` silently skips the write with `// mock astrologer — no real provider to credit`. The 3 genuinely onboarded astrologers appear nowhere in the funnel.
2. **AW-03:** `/astrology/astrologers/live` — the only page that lists real astrologers — is a **hard 404**. The backing API returns the 3 real profiles happily. This makes the go-online toggle decorative.
3. **AW-01:** When a devotee *does* book a real astrologer (₹375, `AC-20260812-628085`), the astrologer can never find it. The dashboard's "Upcoming Sessions" panel is a hardcoded empty state — `upcomingSessions` appears exactly once in the file, inside the TypeScript interface, fetched and never rendered. `/astrologer-portal/consultations` reads a *different*, in-memory data source that is permanently empty.

**Impact:** ₹750 taken for a consultation with a person who does not exist. Every paid consultation with a real astrologer is a guaranteed no-show and a guaranteed refund request.

**Fix:** Point `AstrologyClient.tsx:269` at `/astrology/astrologers/live`; refuse a non-UUID `astrologerId` at `POST /consultation/book`; ship the live directory page; render `api.upcomingSessions` in the dashboard panel and repoint the Consultations page at a DB-backed endpoint (fixing AW-11's `AstrologerProfile.id` vs `User.id` mismatch at the same time).

---

### **P0-8 · Cancelling a refund-eligible consultation refunds the devotee AND still pays the provider**
*AW-06 · Effort: **S***

**Repro:** Book a ₹500 consultation with real astrologer profile `71e54617` (writes Commission `amount=400, PENDING`). Astrologer `/payouts/balance` before: `availableInr: 400`. `POST /consultation/<id>/cancel` → `200 {"cancelled":true,"refundEligible":true}`. Balance after: **`availableInr: 400` — unchanged.** The row is still PENDING and still withdrawable.

**Impact:** Direct cash leakage. Every cancelled consultation is a 100% refund loss *plus* the 80% payout. Also self-dealing-exploitable with a second account.

**Fix:** In the cancel handler, when `refundEligible`, move the Commission for that bookingId to `REVERSED` in the same transaction. The status and the UI styling already exist. Add a regression test asserting the payout balance returns to 0.

---

### **P0-9 · The cookie banner buries the entire login form, and the whole mobile tab bar, on first visit**
*Merges RESP-01 + RESP-02 + RESP-03 · Effort: **S***

**Repro:** Fresh profile (no `darshya_cookie_consent_v1`), viewport 390×844, open `/auth/login`.
Consent card: `top=497 bottom=844` — **347px = 41.2% of the viewport**. Sign In button: `top=649`. `document.elementFromPoint(195, 674)` — the Sign In button's own centre — returns **the "Reject all" button**. The email input is ~90% covered; password, Forgot password, Sign In, Terms, Privacy and Sign Up are all behind the opaque card. It is `position: fixed`, so scrolling does not help. After clicking "Reject all", the same hit test returns Sign In (`intercepted: false`).

Same occlusion at 1440×900 and 768×1024. It also fully covers the `z-40` mobile bottom tab bar (`z-80` over `z-40`, boxes fully nested) — Home/Book/Library/Profile all swallow taps — plus the three homepage category cards, the store category filter row, the puja detail "Book This Puja" CTA, and four admin sidebar links.

**Impact:** A first-time visitor on the most common Indian phone size opens the door to every authenticated flow and sees a cookie wall where the form should be. The tap they will instinctively make lands on "Reject all". Straight signup/login conversion loss.

**Fix:** While open, publish the banner height as a CSS var and reserve it: `body { padding-bottom: var(--consent-h, 0) }`. Make the `<640px` variant a single-row ~72px bar. Raise the tab bar above the banner (`z-[90]`) and offset the banner by the tab height on `<md`. Constrain it to the content column on admin so it never paints over the fixed sidebar.

---

### **P0-10 · Admin cannot move money, and the one money button is dead**
*Merges AO-03 + AO-02 · Effort: **M** (UI only — the APIs all exist and answer 200)*

`/admin/payouts`, `/admin/payout-requests`, `/admin/refunds`, `/admin/bookings`, `/admin/kyc` — **all five return HTTP 404.** No sidebar entry for any of them. Meanwhile, with an admin bearer:
- `GET /admin/bookings/payouts/pending` → 200, three provider groups totalling **₹13,100 owed**
- `GET /admin/bookings/payout-requests` → 200 · `POST .../:id/process` accepts `complete|reject|fail`
- `GET /admin/bookings/refunds/pending` → 200 · `GET /admin/bookings/provider-kyc` → 200

The single money control that *does* exist — "Pay Now" on `/admin/revenue`, in a table headed "Rs. 3,200 due" — is a bare `<button>` with **no onClick**. Instrumented: "any non-GET request fired? **false**". No toast, no dialog, no error. `pendingPayouts` is unchanged afterwards.

**Impact:** Day one, a solo founder cannot pay a single provider or refund a single devotee from the admin panel. The system knows ₹13,100 is owed and exposes an endpoint to settle it; there is simply no screen. An operator clicks Pay Now, sees nothing break, and concludes the payout went through.

**Fix:** Build `/admin/payouts` and `/admin/refunds` against the existing endpoints, add sidebar links, wire Pay Now to `POST /admin/bookings/payout-requests/:id/process` behind a confirm modal. **Until wired, delete the button** — a no-op on a money screen is worse than an absent one.

---

### **P0-11 · Block and Delete on /admin/users report success and persist nothing**
*AO-01 · Effort: **S***

**Repro:** `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/<id> {"blocked":true}` → **200** `{"message":"User updated"}`. Re-read the same user → `{"blocked": false, "blockedAt": null}`. `adminService.updateUser` accepts `blocked` in its signature *and* in the Zod schema but never writes it — only `roles` and `approved` are applied. The columns exist (`schema.prisma:187-189`). The UI mutates local state optimistically, so the row visibly flips to "blocked" or disappears entirely.

**Impact:** This is the platform's only moderation control. An operator blocks a fraudulent pandit, gets a green confirmation, and the account keeps taking bookings and money. Delete is worse — the row vanishes from the admin's view, giving positive evidence the account is gone. The only way to discover the failure is to reload and re-check every action.

**Fix:** Write `{blocked, blockedAt, blockedReason}` in `updateUser`, and return `blocked`/`blockedAt` in the `getUsers` and `getUserById` projections so the Status column reflects reality (this also unblocks AO-08's permanently-empty status filter).

---

### **P0-12 · Headline platform revenue is structurally always ₹0, and three admin screens contradict each other**
*AO-04 · Effort: **S***

`/admin` "Revenue This Month" = **Rs. 0**. `/admin/revenue` "Total Revenue" = **Rs. 0**. `/admin/reports` on the adjacent screen = **Rs. 54.7K + Rs. 4.0K** of puja revenue.

Both zeros sum the `Transaction` table, and `prisma.transaction.create` is called from **exactly one file** — `subscription.service.ts`. No puja booking, store order, donation, consultation or wellness enrolment ever writes a Transaction row. The tile can only ever show subscription revenue.

**Impact:** The founder's single most important number is wrong by construction, and wrong in the direction that hides success — it will read ₹0 on the day the platform takes its first ₹100,000. Every runway, spend and "is this working" decision is made on a fabricated zero.

**Fix:** Point `getDashboard.totalRevenue` and `getRevenue` at `prisma.payment.aggregate({where:{status:'captured'}})` — the source `/admin/metrics` already uses — or at the Commission ledger. `/admin`, `/admin/revenue`, `/admin/reports` and `/admin/metrics` must all read one source.

---

### **P0-13 · Production Supabase service-role credentials sit in the working tree with `NODE_ENV=production`**
*Merges AW-09 + S-12 · Effort: **S** code, **owner-gated** for rotation*

`apps/api/.env` sets `NODE_ENV=production` and points `DATABASE_URL`/`DIRECT_URL` at the live Supabase pooler (project ref `hlkvfarvfblhzhhnhtbp`, `aws-1-ap-northeast-1`), and holds a service-role key, a production JWT secret, and live Groq and Cohere keys. The running API uses `.env.local` (local dev DB) — but **Prisma CLI and a bare `new PrismaClient()` load `.env`, not `.env.local`**.

Confirmed independently by two agents: a script run from `apps/api` returned a *disjoint* set of AstrologerProfile ids from the running API — two different databases. One of them is production, holding 20 temples and 9 demo users. Both agents performed read-only queries against prod before noticing.

**Impact:** One `npx prisma migrate dev`, `prisma db push`, or seed script run from `apps/api` silently targets the live customer database. This is exactly the setup that produces an accidental prod wipe — and it means every local agent session has ambient prod read access, in an environment where agents are instructed to break things on purpose.

**Fix:** Move the Supabase URL into `.env.production` only; make `.env` carry the local dev DB with `NODE_ENV=development`. Add a guard to the prisma scripts that refuses to run when the `DATABASE_URL` host is not localhost without an explicit `--prod` flag. **Owner must rotate** the service-role key, JWT secret, Groq and Cohere keys.

---

### **P0-14 · The mobile app must not ship in its current state**
*Merges PAR-01 … PAR-08 · Effort: **L** · **Contract-verified, not click-verified***

Six independently launch-blocking defects in a binary that cannot be hotfixed:

| # | Defect | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| PAR-01 | **Upgrade grants a ₹5,000/yr ACTIVE subscription with zero payment** and alerts "Upgrade complete." Books a `COMPLETED` ₹5,000 transaction into the admin ledger. | `POST /subscriptions/subscribe` → 200 `status:"ACTIVE"`; `is-premium` → true. Same flaw on web for all non-`astro_monthly` plans. |
| PAR-02 | **My Bookings is always empty**; Profile reads 0 bookings / 0 addresses. | API returns `{bookings:[10], pagination:{total:80}}`; screen reads `.data`. |
| PAR-03/04 | **Priest, Admin and Reports dashboards hard-crash on render.** | `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'toLocaleString')` reproduced in Node against the live payload. The `PriestDashboard`/`AdminDashboard` interfaces are fabrications, so TS passed. Before the crash they flash FALLBACK values of 12,450 users / ₹45.2L revenue — **500× the truth**. |
| PAR-05 | **Live Darshan is 100% dead** — `GET /api/v1/darshan/:id` is a 404; the real route is `/:id/stream`. Re-polled every 15s. | 404 confirmed; `/stream` returns 200 with a playbackUrl. |
| PAR-06 | **Every donation 400s** — the app sends `amount`, the API requires `amountInr`. Hook typed `input: unknown`. | `400 {"fieldErrors":{"amountInr":["Required"]}}` |
| PAR-07 | **Temples/Pujas/Darshan tabs render hardcoded mock catalogues** with prices that contradict the website. Satyanarayan Katha: **₹2,100 in the app, ₹1,501 on the web page and in the API.** Header reads "5 verified temples"; web says 22, API says 20. | Envelope key mismatch on all three (`.data` vs `.temples`/`.pujaTypes`/`.streams`). |

Plus PAR-08: the home-screen panchang is a hardcoded fallback rendering a fabricated tithi **364 days out of 365**, contradicting the website for the same date.

**Fix:** Do not ship. Fix the envelope accessors, delete every mock fallback (a fallback indistinguishable from real data is worse than an empty state), replace every `input: unknown` mutation with a concrete interface, add response schemas to the api-client's existing zod hook so shape drift throws `RESPONSE_SHAPE_MISMATCH` in CI, and gate `/subscribe` behind verified payment.

---

## 3. P1 — FIX IN WEEK ONE

---

**P1-1 · Clicking Pay creates a persistent booking before the gateway opens, and there is no way to pay it** *(DB-03 · M)*
The UI says "we did not open checkout. You have **NOT** been charged" — but `POST /bookings/create-order` already returned 201 and `BS-20260812-QXUWN` persists as `pending_payment`. The same devotee carries **8 ghost bookings** from earlier aborted attempts. They appear under "Upcoming" with only a Cancel button; there is no "Complete payment" control anywhere, and each holds a slot seat for 30 minutes. **Fix:** add a Complete-payment action on `/bookings` and `/bookings/view` (the payment service is already idempotent per booking), exclude `pending_payment` from Upcoming, and correct the failure copy.

**P1-2 · Raw database enum leaks as the status badge on the post-payment screen** *(DB-02 · S)*
Literal on-screen text: `BS-20260812-3GTL7 confirmed …` and eight rows reading `pending_payment` (underscore and all). `getStatusConfig` has 7 keys against a 9-value Prisma enum, is missing `pending_payment`/`confirmed`/`refunded`, and carries a dead `booked` key; the fallback prints the raw enum. The sibling detail page already maps these correctly. This is the first screen a customer sees after paying, reached from the success screen's own "View Booking" CTA.

**P1-3 · Every filter, sort and search control on /store and /pujas is decorative — 15 dead controls** *(merges S-04 + LC-01…05, LC-08 + DB-04 + LC-06/07 · M)*
Both pages are server components; every `<select>` has no `name`, no `value`, no `onChange` and no enclosing `<form>`; every chip has no `onClick`. Drove all 36 `/pujas` dropdown selections and every `/store` option — the card list was **byte-identical** after each. "Under ₹500" still shows a ₹3,499 idol. The active `/store` pill is hardcoded to `i === 0`, so it always reads "All Products". The `/pujas` "Sort: Popularity" button also *mislabels* the current order — the page renders raw catalogue order with no sort anywhere in the file.
**The API already does all of this**: `?sort=price_asc` → 179,199,199,249…; `?q=rudraksha` → 4 of 24; `?collection=rudraksha-malas` → exactly 3. And the `/pujas` search box was already converted to a working GET form — that is the pattern to copy. **This is the founder's reported defect, and it is on both revenue-carrying catalogues.**

**P1-4 · All 24 "Add to Cart" buttons on /store are inert and navigate to the product page instead** *(LC-05 · S)* — bare `<button>` with no handler, nested inside the card's `<a>`, so the click is swallowed by the link. Also **LC-09**: all four "Select Plan" adoption CTAs on `/gaushala` (₹51 / ₹3,000 / ₹30,000 / ₹2,25,000) are dead with no wrapping Link — note TASKS.md #230 records these as already wired, so this looks like a regression or an unmerged fix.

**P1-5 · The pandit portal is unreadable: every date says "Invalid Date" and commission says "-₹NaN"** *(PF-09 + PF-10 · S)*
`new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00')` against an ISO string produces `2026-08-19T00:00:00.000ZT00:00:00` → Invalid Date, on the list, the detail page and the dashboard. And `booking.platformCommission` is never selected by the API, so `formatCurrency(undefined)` renders **"Platform Commission -₹NaN"** on the one screen where a pandit checks the take rate — on a marketplace whose entire pitch is a transparent 20%.

**P1-6 · Video proof: silent-failure success state, no URL validation, and one paste bricks a paid booking permanently** *(PF-03 + PF-06 + DB-09 + PF-05 · M)*
- `onUploaded` is `await fetch(...); setVideoSubmitted(true)` — **no `res.ok` check**. With the API returning 400, the panel renders a green "Video Proof Uploaded / View uploaded video" while the DB holds `videoProofUrl: null`.
- Two spaces are accepted as a proof URL (200). Re-upload then 400s ("only for bookings in completed status"), and delivery 409s (the gate trims to empty). **The booking is permanently stuck** — no video, no payout, repairable only by curl.
- The devotee side trusts it: rows render "Video Ready" / "Watch Video" for empty and malformed URLs.
- The one-tap in-app upload is dead: `POST /video-proof/signed-url` → `400 {"error":"supabaseAdmin.storage.from is not a function"}` — two different definitions of "storage is configured" mean the intended actionable 503 never fires.

**P1-7 · The 48-hour video-proof refund guarantee is enforced nowhere** *(LEG-06 · L)*
`/refunds`, `/nri/ca` and `/why-darshya` all promise a full refund if no video arrives in 48h. The only automation runs at **72h**, only for `status: 'priest_assigned'`, and its own comment says *"⚠ DELIBERATELY NOT writing REFUND_REQUESTED_MARKER here"* — it cancels and drops into a manual queue, issuing nothing. Meanwhile three bookings sit in `completed` with `videoProofUrl: null` and would never be picked up. Video proof is the brand's central promise (`/about`: "No exceptions").

**P1-8 · Devotee reviews never reach the pandit, and a completed booking can never be reviewed** *(PF-11 + DB-08 · S)*
Dashboard rating moved 4.6 (42) → 4.61 (43) after a 5-star review with text; `GET /priest/reviews` returned `{reviews: [], total: 0}` before **and after** — `submitReview` bumps the aggregate but never inserts a `Review` row. Separately, `POST /bookings/:id/review` **409s on `completed`**: "Reviews can only be submitted for bookings in 'delivered' or 'video_uploaded' status." Three of the devotee's bookings sit in `completed` displaying a friendly "Completed" pill and none can be rated. Reviews are the social-proof engine of the marketplace.

**P1-9 · Systemic pagination truncation at 20 across five surfaces** *(merges PF-07, PF-08, AO-07/LC-10, AO-13, AO-11, PF-18 · M)*
Same root everywhere: the client omits `page`/`limit`, the API defaults to 20, and counts are computed from the truncated array.
- Pandit: 20 of 34 shown; tabs say "Accepted 15" against a real 21 and "Completed 5" against 9.
- **PF-08:** the detail page has no by-id endpoint — it fetches the 20-row list and `.find()`s — so **any booking past row 20 cannot be opened at all**, and that is the only screen with the video-proof control.
- Admin users: 20 of 23, footer says "of 20", Pandit filter returns 2 against a real 3.
- **AO-13:** "Export Users" CSV silently drops 3 of 23 — an artefact that leaves the product and goes into a spreadsheet or an accountant's inbox.
- **AO-11:** `/admin/revenue` "Pending Payouts ₹3,200" vs the payout engine's **₹13,100** — the Revenue page only sums PENDING Commission rows and is blind to the entire legacy priest rail (12 completed bookings, ₹9,900), and is additionally capped at `take: 50`.

**P1-10 · Provider earnings labels lie: "Paid out ₹375" when ₹0 is paid and the real earning is ₹300** *(AW-04 + AW-10 + PF-12 · S)*
The astrologer dashboard pipes **gross** revenue into a card labelled "Paid out", passes `pending` as literal `null` (renders "—"), and contradicts the Earnings page on the same portal (`Total ₹700 / Pending ₹700 / Paid out ₹0`) and `/payouts/balance`. Wellness has the same label bug with the right number. The pandit sees "Total Earned ₹2,310" on `/priest/payouts` and `₹0` on `/priest`. **Four screens, four numbers, one booking.**

**P1-11 · The wellness Earnings page is dead — "Invalid query" while ₹800 is pending** *(AW-05 · S)*
`COMMISSION_PAGE_SIZE = 200` in `commission-api.ts` against an API that caps at 100 → `400 pageSize: Number must be less than or equal to 100` on every load. Astrologers hit the same 400. Same page-size reconciliation was missed when the endpoint moved. One-line fix plus a contract test.

**P1-12 · Wellness has a complete supply side and zero demand side** *(AW-07 + AW-13 · L — or a launch-scope decision)*
`/wellness` and all sub-routes are fully static. A grep for `enroll` across `apps/web/app/**/*.tsx` matches only instructor-side pages. The enrolment API works (201 → PAID verified by curl); nothing in the website can invoke it. And there is no `GET /wellness/enrollments/mine` at all, so even a devotee who paid has no way to find their class again. **Either build the catalogue+enrol+my-classes trio, or hide wellness at launch.**

**P1-13 · Store owner, temple manager and gaushala manager portals are hollow — no provider profile row** *(S-05, S-06, S-07, S-08 · M)*
- Store: "Add Product" → `400 {"error":"No StoreProfile for this user"}`, surfaced to the owner verbatim, on an empty state that just told them to add their first product.
- Temple: "Add offering" → `400 {"error":"No matched Temple for this manager"}`. **The slots editor itself is correctly implemented** and stranded behind a broken account linkage that string-matches `templeName` instead of using an FK.
- Gaushala: `profile: null`, all zeros, and **a genuinely paid ₹2,100 donation with a valid receipt is invisible** because donations are recorded against the static directory id `gs_001` rather than a `GaushalaProfile` UUID.
*Partly a seed/environment gap — but the product defects around it are real: raw backend strings shown to users, and GETs returning silent `[]` instead of a "finish setting up your profile" state.*

**P1-14 · Order total silently drops GST and delivery: checkout promises ₹733, the server records and charges ₹698** *(S-09 · S — **must ship UI+backend together**)*
The server explicitly ignores the client total and stores `sum(price*qty)`; GST and delivery are never added server-side, and the Razorpay order is created from that. Latent only because no order can currently be created — it surfaces the instant P0-1 is fixed. Compounded by **LEG-10**: `/terms` §6 says prices are tax-inclusive while the cart adds 5% "GST (est.)" at the last step, and the PDF is headed **"DARSHYA TAX INVOICE"** with no GSTIN anywhere.

**P1-15 · Prasad delivery fee charged with no delivery address** *(DB-05 · S)*
`prasadDelivery: true` with no `deliveryAddress` → **201**, `amount: 130100` (₹150 fee charged), booking persisted with `prasadDeliveryAddressId: null`. No cross-field refinement in the Zod schema; the fee is unconditional while the address is silently dropped. The web wizard validates client-side, so this surfaces as a rare, hard-to-reproduce complaint from the mobile app or any direct API client. Same shape in `POST /from-offering`.

**P1-16 · One devotee can blank a marquee slot for 30 minutes by clicking Pay five times, and bookings are accepted years out** *(DB-07 · S)*
Five identical create-order calls → 5×201 (all same user, same slot), sixth → 409. `GET /slots` correctly reports `remaining: 0`. Capacity is enforced; nothing caps *unpaid holds per user*, and every failed gateway attempt mints another (see P1-1). Separately `scheduledDate: "2031-01-01"` → 201.

**P1-17 · Raw Prisma errors with absolute server paths and source code in 5xx bodies — five routes, five agents** *(merges PF-13, AW-08/PAR-13, AW-12, S-03, SEC-04 · S)*
`400 {"error":"Invalid \`prisma.pujaBooking.count()\` invocation in E:\\NewClaude\\bhaktisetu\\apps\\api\\src\\services\\priest.service.ts:211:26\n 208 skip,\n 209 take: limit,…"}` — filesystem path, file, line number and surrounding source, triggerable by any authenticated pandit with `?status=typo`. Same class on `/store/orders` (500, includes a code comment verbatim), `/astrologer-portal/dashboard` (500, P2023), `/wellness-portal/instructor/mine` (500, `uuid = text`).
**Adjudication:** the security agent rated this P3 on the grounds that `server.ts`'s `setErrorHandler` suppresses `err.message` in production. That is true for errors reaching it — but at least two of these throw before any route-level catch, two more are returned by route-level catches that bypass the handler entirely (`bookings.ts:723`, `:783`), and five agents hit five different leaks. **Raised to P1**: add the missing Zod enums, cast the raw SQL params, and make the global handler authoritative.

**P1-18 · Website muhurat finder 401s for every visitor** *(PAR-14 · S)*
`POST /weddings/muhurat` is behind `authenticateUser`; the web page sends no Authorization header. A high-intent wedding lead fills a 10-field form and gets **"Request failed: 401"**. The mobile hook succeeds because the shared client injects the token — so this is web-only, and it is the top of the highest-value funnel. Either attach the token or drop the auth guard and rate-limit instead (the calculation needs no user context).

**P1-19 · /admin/settings "Save Changes" is a 700ms `setTimeout`** *(AO-10 · M)*
Six commission-rate inputs load hardcoded values, the button shows "Saved" after **0 API calls**, and the field reverts on reload. If a founder discounts a category to onboard supply, they will believe it is live while the ledger charges the old rate. The displayed rates are wrong anyway — Settings says "Astrology 30%" while the live commission row is 20%.

**P1-20 · No admin can open a user record** *(AO-05 + AO-06 · S)*
Every name link and Eye icon points at `/admin/users/<uuid>` → **404** (no `[id]` segment exists). The only detail route, `/admin/users/view?id=`, is **backed by a hardcoded mock map** and answers *"User not found. This may be a demo limitation."* for real seeded users. 24 dead links on the first page of the table alone, on the entry point for every support and moderation task.

**P1-21 · Provider price list on /pricing is fiction** *(LEG-04 + LEG-05 + LEG-08 · M)*
`/pricing` sells ₹499 / ₹1,499 / ₹4,999 plus a 4-tier commission ladder. The API sells ₹999 / ₹2,999 / ₹799 / ₹1,999 / ₹1,499 / ₹3,999 / ₹699 / ₹4,999, has **no Enterprise plan** for pandit/astrologer/store/wellness, and applies a **flat 20%** with no plan lookup anywhere in the codebase. `/terms` §7 publishes a *third* schedule and cites "standard MedusaJS take rate" for a store that runs on Prisma. `/faqs` sells NRI Premium with **"4 free pujas per year"** — worth more than the ₹5,000 subscription — against an API plan whose features list contains none of the five things promised.

**P1-22 · Astrologer demo account is dead and leaks stack traces** *(AW-08 / PAR-13 · S)*
`astro@demo.com` logs in with a synthetic non-UUID id `demo-astro`; every DB-backed route then 500s with `P2023 Error creating UUID … found 'm' at 3` and the source path. This is the account used for investor demos. The middleware should 401 when the demo handle's User row is missing rather than synthesising an id.

**P1-23 · No named grievance officer, no legal entity, no GSTIN, no registered address** *(LEG-11 + LEG-10 · S code, owner-gated content)*
`/privacy` promises a 48h/30-day grievance SLA; `/refunds` promises 15 business days; neither names the officer. IT Rules 2021 Rule 3(2) and the E-Commerce Rules 2020 Rule 4(5) both require name, designation and contact. `postalContactLine()` returns null everywhere because `IS_INCORPORATED_VERIFIED: false` — the only identity published is "Bengaluru, India."

**P1-24 · /csr-match publicly claims "Bhaktisetu is 80G certified + CSR-1 registered"** *(LEG-14 · S)*
Reachable, 200, indexable. `company.ts` says neither is verified. The file's own "dev-server only" comment is not a control. Aimed at corporates routing CSR spend, whose own compliance depends on the claim. **Gate behind an env flag defaulting off, or delete.**

**P1-25 · Mobile store, booking, cart, settings and reports are decorative** *(PAR-15…PAR-20 · L — contract-verified)*
"Add to cart" opens a **browser search** for the product name; the create-booking hook points at `POST /api/v1/bookings` which **404s** (real route is `/bookings/book`); temple deep links use the id against a `[slug]` route so every one 404s; the yatra CTA opens WhatsApp to **919999999999**; Admin Settings feature-flag switches (including "Video Proof Required" and payment gateways) are `useState` only; Admin Reports "Generate" fires no request and says "you will receive an email shortly."

## 4. P2 / P3 — BACKLOG

**Design system (the single highest-leverage cluster — four token renames in one file fix the biggest defect)**
- `--font-family-sans/serif` are declared under names Tailwind v4 never reads; measured `--font-family-sans` = **empty string**, `--font-serif` = Tailwind stock. 579 `font-serif`/`font-mono` call sites render in OS fallback — including every Sanskrit shloka and every receipt number. Crimson Pro is downloaded on every page and used only in the book reader. *(DU-01)*
- `shadow-warm*` is an undefined utility: 49 usages render `box-shadow: none`, including the sign-in card. *(DU-02)*
- Cabinet Grotesk is homepage-only — 6 of 10 screens render zero of it while still paying the third-party CDN download. *(DU-06)*
- Body font falls back to `Inter`, which is never loaded; the self-hosted Noto Sans that **is** loaded is referenced nowhere. Devanagari has no working fallback. *(DU-11)*
- Two "primary" colours ship side by side (`#B87333` and `#9E6229`) plus a gradient of both; 16 distinct warm hexes including two stock Tailwind oranges in no token. *(DU-04, DU-09)*
- 2,128 cool-gray utilities on a warm-cream brand; 1,345 `bg-white` vs 653 warm surface cards. *(DU-12)*
- Body copy spans 12px→18px in five styles; two competing base scales in `globals.css`; 722 arbitrary `text-[Npx]`, 102 of them 9px. *(DU-10)*
- 13 radius utilities over 888 uses against a 6-token scale; 11 container widths over 679 declarations. *(DU-15, DU-14)*
- The puja error boundary is the only screen in system-ui, with an off-scale 8px radius, inline hex literals and no `h1`. *(DU-13)*

**Heading & semantic structure (SEO + a11y)**
- 349 `h1` tags in 45 size+weight recipes; `h1` ranges 18px→70.4px across 7 treatments; `/auth/login` and the puja error state have **no h1**; `/priest` has two. *(DU-05)*
- An `h3` renders at 36px on `/pujas` while an `h2` on the same page is 16px; 811 `h2` in 37 recipes. *(DU-07)*
- `/temples`, `/store` and `/priest` skip h1→h3 with 21–24 consecutive same-level card titles and no grouping h2. *(DU-08)*

**Responsive & touch**
- 17–44 sub-44px tap targets per page at 390px: 16×16 password reveal (no accessible name), 20×20 portal sidebar close, 48×28 on-duty toggle, three adjacent 36×36 header controls, 14.8px-tall "Save"/"Share"/"Forgot password?" links, five 18px language links on one footer row. *(RESP-06…09, RESP-11)*
- 190 visible elements under 12px on the mobile homepage, floor 9px — including "Aadhaar authenticated" and price/rating/delivery copy. `--text-xs` only reaches 12px at ~960px viewport width. *(RESP-05)*
- Homepage "Next festival" strip collides at 390px: the CTA pill prints over the festival name. *(RESP-04)*
- Provider/admin portals stack two headers **plus** the consumer bottom tab bar = 177–185px of chrome (21.9% of the viewport), with a shopping cart icon shown to a signed-in admin. *(RESP-10)*

**List/URL state & discoverability**
- `/temples` and `/astrology` filter/sort state never reaches the URL — the read path works (`?deity=Vishnu` → 9) but the write path never fires, so no filtered view is shareable, bookmarkable, back-button-recoverable or indexable. *(LC-12, AW-16)*
- `/astrology` silently substitutes 8 hardcoded fallback astrologers when the fetch fails, presenting fabricated practitioners as bookable — so even a correct sort operates on the wrong universe. **Adjudication:** this is a *second, distinct* layer of fakery from P0-7's 15 API fixtures — one client-side, one server-side. Both are real. *(LC-13)*
- `/blog` renders zero posts; `/gurukuls` renders zero results with perfectly wired filters; `/wellness/instructors` and `/profile/bookings` 404. *(LC-14, LC-15, AW-17)*
- `/store-admin/orders` tab counts ignore the search box. *(LC-16)* · `/temples` "Reset filters" does not reset the sort. *(LC-17)*

**Admin data integrity**
- `/admin/subscriptions` is hardcoded `[]` / `active: 0` while `/admin/revenue` and `/admin/dashboard` both report 6–7 active subs from a table that exists. *(AO-09)*
- `/admin/metrics` reports "0 signups today" for the first 5.5 hours of every IST day (UTC vs `en-IN` day boundary) while `/admin` lists 10 signups dated today. *(AO-14)*
- `/admin/reports` Popular Pujas sums to 54 bookings under a "Total Bookings 53" tile, and its "revenue" includes unpaid and cancelled rows. *(AO-15)*
- **Five admin CRUD endpoints are stubs that return fake success**: `GET /admin/temples` → `[]` despite 20 seeded; `PATCH`/`DELETE /admin/temples/<nonexistent-uuid>` → **200 "Temple deleted"**; `POST /admin/users` → 200 with no user created. No page consumes them today — which is the only reason this is not P0. *(AO-16)*
- Raw UUIDs and blank cells in `/admin/revenue`'s Provider and Booking columns — on the screen where money is authorised — while `/admin/commissions` renders the identical rows correctly. *(AO-12, AO-17)*
- `GET /admin/bookings?status=NOT_A_STATUS` → 200 with `[]` instead of 400; and note the trap for whoever builds the payouts UI: `payout-requests` wants lowercase statuses, `commissions` wants uppercase. *(AO-18)*

**Legal / content**
- `/shipping` publishes a ₹999 free-delivery threshold and weight-based fees; the cart uses ₹499 and a flat ₹49. *(LEG-12)*
- `/about` claims "200+ Temples partnered" against 20, and "100% Bookings with video proof" against three `completed` bookings with `videoProofUrl: null`. *(LEG-13)*
- No way to withdraw cookie consent — the policy instructs users to hand-delete a localStorage key, which is self-documenting GDPR/DPDP non-compliance. *(LEG-15)*
- A working DSAR endpoint (`POST /api/v1/dpdp/dsar`) exists and is wired to no UI; a grep of `apps/web` for `dpdp` returns zero hits. Rights are email-only, to inboxes the codebase's own comment calls "aspirational". *(LEG-16)*
- A permanently-live "Akshaya Tritiya Sale — offer valid till April 30" with permanent struck-through MRPs, four months later. *(LEG-17)*
- `/refunds` instructs users to click a "Request Refund" button that does not exist, on a page that failed to load twice with a valid session. *(LEG-18)*
- Four different refund settlement timelines across four pages. *(LEG-19)*
- `/nri-pujas` advertises 7-currency checkout with "no markup"; the product is INR-only via Razorpay and Terms says so. *(LEG-09)* — note `/uk` and `/nri/ca` handle this honestly, so the correct copy already exists.

**Other**
- Pandit take rate is **70%, not the 80% the pitch promises** — `seed.ts:934` writes `commissionRate: 0.70` while `schema.prisma:334` now defaults to 0.80 and `DEFAULT_PRIEST_SHARE = 0.8`. ₹110 less on every ₹1,100 booking. Owner decision + backfill. *(PF-16)*
- On-duty toggle never reflects saved state, so a pandit going off-duty must first click **on** — re-arming themselves for 1-minute-SLA offers. *(PF-14)*
- API failures render as "You have no assigned bookings yet" / "0 sessions" — every provider is told they have no work during any hiccup, invisibly to the founder. *(PF-15, AW-18, AW-15)*
- Nested `<a>` hydration error on the pandit bookings list makes hit areas unreliable. *(PF-17)*
- Two payout rails, one navigable — the pandit sidebar points at the legacy rail while real paid bookings settle on the unified one. *(PF-19)*
- Dev CORS allowlist omits `127.0.0.1:3000`. **Adjudication:** the responsive agent chose not to file this as a product defect and was right — no prod impact. But it caused **four** agents to mis-measure (portals rendering confident zeros, phantom 404s), so file it as a P3 dev-experience fix. It also exposes a real product gap: **portal dashboards have no error state and render zeros on fetch failure.** *(AW-15, S-13)*
- `X-Powered-By: Next.js` on every response. *(SEC-03)*
- App/web scope gap: astrologers and wellness instructors have **no earnings screen at all** on mobile; temple managers have no bookings or payouts; the home search bar is a disguised tab link; ~150 web content routes vs one gated knowledge screen; 11 flows are `Linking.openURL` bounce-outs that drop the session and back-stack. *(PAR-22)*
- The same darshan stream reports `live` from `/live` and `offline` from `/:id/stream`, with two different playback URLs. *(PAR-21)*

## 5. WHAT WORKS — verified by a real click

This is the honest, marketable core. Every item below was driven in a real browser or against the live API.

**The money arithmetic is genuinely excellent.** The entire revenue path was driven in the UI and every price agreed at every screen: `/pujas` listing ₹1,100 → detail ₹1,100 → temple card ₹1,100 → Review breakdown (₹1,100 + ₹51) → Payment step "Total Amount ₹1,151" → button "Pay ₹1,151 with Razorpay" → `POST /bookings/create-order` returned **`amount: 115100`** paise. Prasad adds exactly ₹150 on both client and server. Booking numbers are real, server-issued, `BS-YYYYMMDD-XXXXX`, collision-checked.

**The 20/80 take rate holds exactly** where the ledger is used. Verified independently on astrology (₹500 gross → ₹100 platform cut → ₹400 provider, `ratePercent: 20`) and wellness (₹1,000 → ₹200 → ₹800), agreeing across the admin ledger, the provider earnings page and `/payouts/commissions`. A real ₹2,500 wellness session created, enrolled and paid appeared in `/admin/commissions` immediately as a correct PENDING row.

**Slot capacity is genuinely enforced.** Six identical create-order calls on one offering+date+slot → 5×201 then a clean `409 SLOT_UNAVAILABLE`, with `GET /slots` correctly reporting 5→0 while the other six slots stayed at 5.

**Server-side validation is strong.** Past dates 400, empty or 6+ devotees 400, bad pincode/phone 400, wrong puja-for-offering 400, unknown slug 404, no-auth 401, double-cancel 409, reschedule-to-past 409, review rating out of range 400. Client-side messages are clear and specific ("Name is required", "Please agree to the terms & conditions").

**Authorization is airtight.** A forged devotee token was thrown at **111 admin routes** (GET plus every mutation) — all 401/403. A devotee's real booking was read and PATCHed as pandit, astrologer, store, temple, wellness, gaushala and admin — all **403 "Not your booking"**. Payout endpoints reject DEVOTEE and scope balance/commissions to the caller's own userId with no self-approve surface. Conversations are participant-only. Forged email-session tokens, garbage bearers and missing tokens all fail closed. CORS returns no ACAO for an evil origin. 25 wrong-password attempts → 429 after 10.

**Business rule 1 is correctly enforced.** `PATCH` to `delivered` and to `video_uploaded` without proof both refuse with `409 {"error":"Video proof required"}`. And when `delivered` *is* reached properly, the payout is correct: `PriestPayout` amount "770", pending, bank_transfer, linked to the right booking.

**Donations run clean end to end.** ₹2,100 → `create-order` (210000 paise) → `verify` → `DR-20260812-D287` in the documented format. *(The 80G wording on the resulting PDF is the P0 — the payment rail itself is sound.)*

**Layout engineering is solid.** **Zero horizontal overflow across 30 page × viewport combinations** — `scrollWidth === clientWidth` on every one, `scrollX` still 0 after `scrollTo(99999, 0)`. Admin tables wrap in `overflow-x-auto` and drop columns responsively. Inputs are forced to `max(16px, 1rem)` so iOS will not focus-zoom. The mobile drawer opens, scroll-locks, closes on Escape and re-toggles. The bottom tab bar is correctly suppressed on `/auth/*` and `/checkout/*`.

**Five list pages are genuinely well built.** `/temples` (state select, A→Z and By-State sorts, five deity chips, Live Darshan toggle 33→27, search "Tirupati"→2, honest zero-state), `/astrology` (price sort verified monotonic 20,25,40,45,50,55,60,80; six specialization chips; online-only 16→14), `/yatra` (all three selects correct), `/darshan`, `/gurukuls`. `/pujas` free-text search works correctly with a proper zero-state and preserves the query across load-more.

**The admin shell is good.** All 18 `/admin` routes return 200, render their heading, and produced **zero console errors and zero failed API requests** across a full sweep. The approvals queue works end to end (PATCH → dashboard count 0→1 → card appears → click Approve → "All caught up!" → back to 0). `/admin/commissions` is a well-built ledger with correct gross / platform cut / provider earning / provider name and working status chips. All three CSV exports produce files with real headers. `/admin`, `/admin/users` and `/admin/revenue` render clean at 390×844.

**The cookie banner's *behaviour* is correct** (its geometry is not): no analytics or marketing request fires before consent, and Reject/Accept are equal weight. The DPDP privacy policy's substantive drafting is solid.

**The booking success screen is well designed** (code-reviewed — payment could not complete on this stack): booking number, amount, video-proof next step, View Booking CTA. And the confirmation screen for astrology consultations *was* driven live and is genuinely good.

## 6. BY ROLE — can this person do their core job today?

| Role | Verdict |
|---|---|
| **Devotee** | **Partially.** Can browse, book and reach the Pay button with correct money at every step — then cannot find the booking in My Bookings (P0-4), sees a raw `pending_payment` enum where the status should be, cannot filter or sort either catalogue, and cannot buy anything from the store at all. |
| **Pandit** | **No.** Cannot click Accept, Start or Mark Complete — the first click 400s. Every date reads "Invalid Date", commission reads "-₹NaN", 6 bookings with video proof show ₹0 earned, and no booking past row 20 will even open. |
| **Astrologer** | **No.** The demo account 500s on every route; a real astrologer is invisible in the consumer funnel, can never see or join a consultation they were paid for, and reads "Paid out ₹375" against a real ₹0. |
| **Wellness instructor** | **No.** Can create classes, set a room link and see students — but no devotee can discover or enrol in anything, and the Earnings page renders "Invalid query" against a real ₹800. Complete supply side, zero demand side. |
| **Temple manager** | **No.** Cannot create a single offering (`400 No matched Temple for this manager`), so the correctly-built slots editor can never make anything bookable. Portal shows `profile: null` and all zeros. |
| **Store owner** | **No.** Cannot add a product (`400 No StoreProfile for this user`), has no orders to fulfil because no order can be created, and the shopper catalogue is 24 unpurchasable fixtures. |
| **Gaushala manager** | **No.** A genuinely paid ₹2,100 donation with a valid receipt shows as `{"donations":[]}` and `totalDonationsInr: 0`. Every screen is zeros with no explanation. |
| **Admin** | **Partially.** Can log in, reach all 18 screens with zero errors, approve a provider, and read a correct commission ledger. Cannot pay, refund, review KYC, block, delete, open a user, save a setting, or trust the revenue number on the landing page. |

**One of eight roles can complete a meaningful job today.**

## 7. DESIGN & UX

### Quantified consistency findings

| Dimension | Measured divergence |
|---|---|
| **Primary CTA** | **11 pixel heights** (31.2 / 33.2 / 35.2 / 36 / 39.2 / 40.8 / 42 / 42.4 / 44 / 49.6 / 56px), **11 padding sets**, **4 radii** (8 / 10 / 16 / pill), **3 fills**, **5 type styles** → **15 distinct full recipes in one 8-page capture**. Repo-wide: 297 `bg-saffron-600` elements in 85 class recipes, 26 paddings, 6 radius utilities. **There is no shared Button component anywhere in `apps/web/components`.** |
| **Primary colour** | **Two** are shipping simultaneously — `#B87333` and `#9E6229` — on the *same* homepage ("Explore Temples" vs "Get free panchang"), plus a gradient of both on the header CTA. The token comments label the wrong one as primary: `bg-saffron-600` (the "hover" token) appears 339× vs `bg-saffron-500` at 53×. |
| **Warm palette** | 16 distinct warm hexes rendered across 10 screens; only 6 belong to the ramp. Two — `#E16318` and `#9E3F0A`, on 30 homepage elements — are stock Tailwind orange in **no token at all**. 24 warm background tokens over 1,680 uses. |
| **Typography** | `--font-family-sans` resolves to the **empty string** at runtime; `--font-serif` resolves to Tailwind stock → **579 `font-serif`/`font-mono` call sites render in OS fallback**. `h1` spans 18px→70.4px (**3.9×**) in 7 treatments across 2 typefaces; 349 `h1` in 45 recipes, 811 `h2` in 37. Body copy 12px→18px in 5 styles from two competing base scales. 722 arbitrary `text-[Npx]`, 102 of them 9px. |
| **Shadows / radii / containers** | 49 `shadow-warm*` usages render `box-shadow: none` (measured on the sign-in card). 13 radius utilities over 888 uses against a 6-token scale. 11 container widths over 679 declarations. |
| **Neutrals** | **2,128 cool-gray/slate/zinc utilities** across 122 files on a warm-cream brand; 1,345 `bg-white` vs 653 warm surface cards, producing a visible seam between adjacent cards on one page. |

### Responsive breakages
Zero horizontal overflow anywhere — the underlying layout is right. The failures are z-order, offset and touch size: the 347px consent card burying login and navigation on first visit (P0-9); 17–44 sub-44px targets per page down to a 16×16 unlabelled password toggle; 190 elements under 12px on the mobile homepage with a 9px floor, on a product whose audience skews older; 177–185px of stacked chrome on the provider/admin portals; the homepage festival strip colliding at 390px.

### Highest-leverage UX changes for conversion, in order

1. **Offset the consent banner** (one component, ~1h) — unblocks the login form, the bottom tab bar, three homepage cards, the store filter row, the puja Book CTA and four admin nav links, at all three viewports, on every first visit.
2. **Wire the `/store` and `/pujas` filter bars** — 15 controls on the two revenue-carrying catalogues currently do nothing. The API already implements every one of them, and `/pujas`'s own search box is the working pattern to copy. A shopper who filters "Hanuman puja under ₹500", sees an unchanged list including ₹3,501 items, and leaves is the single largest silent abandonment in the funnel.
3. **Fix My Bookings** (pagination + status labels) — the post-payment screen is the highest-trust moment in the funnel, and it currently either hides the order or greets it with a grey lowercase `confirmed`.
4. **Rename four font tokens in `globals.css`** (~30 min) — the largest single design defect in the product, fixed in one file. Add `--shadow-warm*` in the same commit.
5. **Ship a `Button` primitive** with 3 sizes / 4 variants and migrate the 10 funnel screens first — this is the loudest "stitched together by different people" signal, and it sits directly on the conversion path.
6. **Add a "Complete payment" CTA** on `pending_payment` bookings — every abandoned checkout is currently unrecoverable revenue with no resume path at all.
7. **Raise `--text-xs`'s clamp floor and bump tap targets to 44px** — trust copy ("Aadhaar authenticated", prices, ratings) is currently the least readable text on the page.

---

## 8. SECURITY, LEGAL, PARITY

### Security — **79/100, the strongest area, and the score is earned**
Adversarial probing found no authz holes: 111 admin routes against a forged devotee token, cross-role IDOR on a real booking across all 7 non-owner roles, provider-portal probes, payout self-approval attempts, participant-only messaging, store-order ownership, forged email-session tokens, SQLi in `?search`, NoSQL type confusion, malformed UUIDs, CORS origin spoofing, and login brute-force — **all correctly rejected.**

Four material risks:
1. **Prod service-role credentials in the working tree with `NODE_ENV=production`** (P0-13). Owner must rotate.
2. **No security headers on any HTML response.** CSP, HSTS, X-Frame-Options, nosniff, Referrer-Policy and Permissions-Policy live only in `apps/web/public/_headers` — a **Cloudflare Pages convention that the current Render Next-standalone host does not honor**. Proof that `headers()` *is* active but simply omits them: `/robots.txt` returns the exact `Cache-Control` from `next.config.ts`, while `/auth/login` returns no security header at all. *Production was deliberately not probed this run — this needs an owner-run `curl -sI https://darshya.online/auth/login`.*
3. **Auth token in localStorage** (`poojly_session`), so any XSS is full account takeover — compounding with (2), since there is no CSP to contain an XSS. An httpOnly cookie path (`darshya_session`) already exists and the API already accepts it; the localStorage copy just needs retiring on web.
4. **Raw Prisma errors with absolute paths and source lines in 5xx bodies** on five separate routes (P1-17).

### Legal — **the most dangerous area in the product, and the cheapest to fix**
Ranked by exposure: the **false 80G tax receipt** (P0-5) creates liability for the donor *and* the platform on every donation. The **four contradictory refund ladders** (P0-6) are a provable Consumer Protection Act claim built from the site's own text, in both directions. The **CSR-1 claim** to corporate donors is worse per-incident than the consumer 80G issue because the corporate's own compliance depends on it. **No legal entity, GSTIN, address or named grievance officer** anywhere, while the platform issues a PDF headed "DARSHYA TAX INVOICE" and adds an on-top 5% "GST (est.)" to prices Terms declares tax-inclusive — that combination is both a tax problem and a textbook CCPA drip-pricing finding. The unenforceable 48h video-proof guarantee, the invented "4 free pujas per year" NRI benefit, the fictional provider price list, the ₹999-vs-₹499 shipping contradiction, the "200+ temples" claim against 20, and a four-months-expired sale with permanent struck-through MRPs round it out.

**Almost all of this is copy, not engineering.** A single focused day of legal-copy work, plus one conditional in the PDF generator and one env gate, removes the majority of the statutory exposure.

### Parity — mobile hooks calling routes that do not exist

**Routes referenced by shipped app code that return 404:**

| Constant / hook | Path called | Reality |
|---|---|---|
| `DARSHAN_STREAM` → `useDarshanStream` | `GET /api/v1/darshan/:id` | **404.** Real route is `/:id/stream`. Live Darshan is 100% dead and re-polls the 404 every 15s. |
| `BOOKINGS` → `useCreateBooking` | `POST /api/v1/bookings` | **404.** Real route is `/bookings/book`. The hook is dead on arrival. |
| `PAYMENT_BY_BOOKING` | `GET /api/v1/payments/booking/:id` | **404.** Real route is `/payments/:id`. |

**Routes that exist but are called with the wrong contract:** `POST /donations/create-order` (`amount` vs required `amountInr` → 400 on every donation, hook typed `input: unknown`); `POST /subscriptions/subscribe` (grants a paid plan with no payment).

**Twelve screens read a response key the API never returns** (`.data` on `{temples}` / `{pujaTypes}` / `{streams}` / `{bookings}` / `{addresses}` / `{sessions}` / flat panchang / flat priest profile) and silently fall through to hardcoded mock catalogues. **Three screens hard-crash** on `toLocaleString` because the `PriestDashboard` and `AdminDashboard` TypeScript interfaces are fabrications that never matched the payload — TypeScript gave false confidence throughout.

The structural lesson: `packages/api-client` already supports zod response validation (`client.ts:215`) and **no caller passes a schema**. Adding one per list hook converts every finding in this section into a CI failure.

---

## 9. THE PLAN

Effort: **S** ≤ 2h · **M** ≤ 2 days · **L** 3+ days. ⚠ = **UI change that must ship paired with a backend change.**

### WAVE 1 — before any paid customer (≈ 9–12 dev-days)
*Goal: one customer can pay for one puja, one pandit can deliver it, one pandit gets paid, and nothing on the site is a lie.*

| # | Item | Effort | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Remove the Content-Type header from the 3 bodyless pandit PATCHes | **S** | One line, two files. Unblocks the entire supply side. Do this first. |
| 2 | `createPriestPayout` on video-proof upload + "Confirm delivery" / "Mark delivered" control | **S**+**S** | Backend, then UI-only (API already authorises both parties). |
| 3 | `/bookings` — request `limit=100`, derive tab counts from API `total` | **S** | UI-only. |
| 4 | `/bookings` — add the 3 missing status keys, drop the dead `booked` key, type the map against the enum | **S** | UI-only. Reuse the labels the detail page already has. |
| 5 | Store checkout: `line2: z.string().nullish()`, UUID guard on the product lookup, friendly error copy | **M** | Backend + UI. |
| 6 | Unify the store catalogue — back `/store/products` with `StoreProduct` rows | **L** | ⚠ Must land with #5 and #10 or checkout stays broken. |
| 7 | Gate the 80G header + eligibility line on `regn_80g && pan`; retitle to "DONATION RECEIPT" otherwise | **S** | ⚠ Pair with removing 80G advertising on `/donations` and `/gaushala`. |
| 8 | Pick **one** refund ladder; rewrite `/refunds`, `/terms` §8, `/faqs`, `/nri-pujas`, `/why-darshya`, and both trust badges | **S** | ⚠ Best paired with a public policy endpoint so copy renders from `DEFAULT_CANCEL_POLICY`. |
| 9 | Reverse the Commission to `REVERSED` inside the cancel transaction | **S** | Backend. Add the regression test. |
| 10 | Compute GST + delivery server-side; assert client total matches | **S** | ⚠ **Must ship in the same commit as #5/#6** or you charge the wrong amount. |
| 11 | Consent banner: reserve height on `<body>`, single-row `<640px`, raise tab bar to `z-[90]` | **S** | UI-only. |
| 12 | Move prod secrets out of `apps/api/.env`; add a non-localhost DB guard to the prisma scripts | **S** | Then **owner rotates keys** (see §10). |
| 13 | Astrology: point the directory at `/astrologers/live`, ship the live directory page, refuse non-UUID `astrologerId`, render `upcomingSessions` | **M** | ⚠ UI + backend together. |
| 14 | Decide wellness + astrology launch scope; **hide** whichever is not being built | **S** | See §10. |
| 15 | Admin: write `blocked`/`blockedAt` in `updateUser` and return them in the projections | **S** | Backend. |
| 16 | Admin: build `/admin/payouts` + `/admin/refunds` and wire Pay Now | **M** | UI-only — every endpoint already exists and answers 200. |
| 17 | Point the revenue tile at captured Payments; make `/admin`, `/admin/revenue`, `/admin/reports`, `/admin/metrics` read one source | **S** | Backend. |
| 18 | `/csr-match` behind `ENABLE_CSR_MATCH` defaulting off | **S** | |
| 19 | Add a `<title>`-level decision: **do not ship the mobile app** | — | Pull it from any launch comms. |

### WAVE 2 — week one (≈ 8–10 dev-days)
*Goal: the product stops embarrassing itself and an operator can run it.*

| # | Item | Effort |
|---|---|---|
| 20 | Wire the `/store` and `/pujas` filter/sort/search bars via GET forms + server-side filters ⚠ (needs the live catalogue from #6) | **M** |
| 21 | "Complete payment" CTA on `pending_payment`; exclude them from Upcoming; correct the failure copy | **M** |
| 22 | Systemic pagination sweep: pandit list, pandit `GET /priest/bookings/:id`, admin users (server-side filters + real `total`), CSV exports, payouts, `/admin/revenue` pending-payouts source | **M** |
| 23 | Pandit portal data fixes: date parsing (3 files), `platformCommission` in the select, on-duty state from the API, error states distinct from empty states | **S** |
| 24 | Video proof hardening: `res.ok` check, `z.string().trim().url()`, allow re-upload while `video_uploaded`, fix the storage-config guard to return 503 | **M** |
| 25 | Reviews: insert a `Review` row in `submitReview` + backfill; allow `completed` in the review handler | **S** |
| 26 | EarningsCard: real paid/pending split, correct labels, one definition across dashboard/payouts/balance (astrologer + wellness + pandit) | **S** |
| 27 | `COMMISSION_PAGE_SIZE` → 100 + a contract test that the client default is accepted by both routes | **S** |
| 28 | Prasad-without-address refinement; per-user unpaid-hold cap; max booking horizon | **S** |
| 29 | Error hygiene: Zod enums on `status` params, `::uuid` casts, route-level catches routed through the global sanitiser | **S** |
| 30 | Fix the muhurat 401 (attach token or drop the guard + rate-limit) | **S** |
| 31 | Admin: `/admin/users/[id]` (or repoint the hrefs) + replace the mock detail page with a real fetch | **S** |
| 32 | Admin settings: real GET/PUT backed by config, or make the fields read-only and delete the Save button | **M** |
| 33 | Provider profile linkage: seed `StoreProfile` / `TempleProfile` / `GaushalaProfile` for the demo+real managers, replace the `templeName` string match with an FK, add "finish setup" empty states ⚠ | **M** |
| 34 | Legal copy sweep: pricing from the API, Terms §7, shipping thresholds, NRI features, `/about` stats from live counts, expired sale, grievance officer block, entity/GSTIN block | **M** |
| 35 | Cookie-consent withdrawal link + `/account/privacy` wired to the existing DSAR endpoint | **M** |
